# Colored HOMFLY polynomials H_R(A, q) of the Borromean rings L6a4 = closure of the
#   3-braid (s1 s2^-1)^3, all three components colored R.
# Convention: A^{-1}H(L+) - A H(L-) = (q - 1/q) H(L0); H_R(unknot) = 1 (reduced:
#   unnormalised invariant / dim_q R); no framing factor needed (all linking numbers 0).
# H_R = N / den with den = prod over boxes of R of (q^h - q^-h)^2, h = hook length;
#   the fraction is reduced.  Checks: (A, q) -> (1/A, 1/q) symmetry; R = [1] at A = q^2
#   gives the Jones polynomial with t = q^2; H_{R^T}(A, q) = H_R(A, -1/q); at q = 1
#   H_R / H_[1]^|R| = (dim R / |R|!)^2, i.e. N_R(A, 1) = N_[1](A, 1)^|R|.
# Computed directly: one R of each pair {R, R^T}; the other is derived by transposition.
# Method: H_R = sum_Q dim_q(Q) Tr_Q (R1 R2^-1)^3 / dim_q(R) over R x R x R -> Q, inclusive
#   Racah matrices numerically at each q (scripts/borromean_pts.py); exact integer traces
#   from two primes (scripts/borromean_exact.py), H by dense interpolation modulo several
#   primes with a stable CRT lift (scripts/borromean_assemble.py).

R = [1]
